# Statistical relationship inventory — PASS 1 primary shard

Runtime agent: `/root/statistical_pass1`; run model `gpt-6.1-sol`; reasoning effort `high`; start mode `FRESH_SPAWN`. Scope: every main-paper inferential relationship, every protocol inferential/design/power relationship, report eTables2/4 and all report eFigures/models. Independent extra shard owns only eTables3/5/6: S121-S222. Numeric namespace 101 through 120 remains unassigned and contains no omitted evidence. This artifact owns S001-S100; the coordinator performs the canonical merge. All source roles below are assigned by content: DOC001 main REPORT, DOC002 protocol PLAN, DOC003 results/methods supplement REPORT; DOC004 data-sharing page REPORT contains no inferential relationship.

Read QUALITY_CONTROL_SCOPE.md and workflow_1_6_2/review_contract.md completely. Inputs are exact supplied PDFs, new evidence maps and source-linked usable locators, with no earlier findings or conclusions. Direct Poppler26.01.0 CPU extraction produced `statistics/protocol_statistical_direct.txt` (DOC002 pp26-36) and `statistics/supplement_outcomes_direct.txt` (DOC003 pp15-18). Commands: `pdftotext -layout -f 26 -l 36 joi240004supp1_prod_1721756962.80052.pdf .../statistics/protocol_statistical_direct.txt` and `pdftotext -layout -f 15 -l 18 joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf .../statistics/supplement_outcomes_direct.txt`. No OCR/fallback environment/new rendering. Visually inspected source-linked images DOC001 p6 and fresh mapper images DOC002 pp26/28; protocol formulas/power illustrations additionally have complete visual confirmation in the new support map. Exact direct native pages supplied the other stated text.

Table row records contain every group estimate and interval, together with the between-group test: they do not collapse independently tested outcomes. Genuine repeated occurrences are cross-referenced. Every record checks all estimates in that row for ordered endpoints and containment, and checks its corresponding group CI/test. Limits on exact test reconstruction are named, rather than silently filling in degrees of freedom, sidedness, covariance or estimator. All CIs below are printed95% unless specified; parentheses for raw values are SD and square brackets for raw medians are IQR. Medical M; surgery B. No candidate IDs or adjudications are assigned.

## Shared row check specification

MainTable2 headers M baseline96/year7 82 and B baseline166/year7 136 are displayed headers, not outcome-specific observed denominators. eTable2 has no outcome-specific denominators; eTable1 separately describes130 participants reaching12years. Raw summaries and model estimates differ by design. Continuous group contrasts are surgery change minus medical change (null0); within-group fold changes and all odds ratios have null1. Every point is within its ordered interval. Every numerical group CI excludes/includes its null consistently with its own printed P at.05, including nonsignificant rows; year12 remission has no group CI to test. No inferential conclusion is generated from raw IQR/SD ranges. All significance conclusions and effect directions reconcile within each displayed row. Exact P reconstruction is limited by absent reported test distribution/degrees of freedom; normal z values from interval width are diagnostic only. MainTable2 binary log-interval symmetry has one reproduced exception S013/F-ST1-007; every other binary cell permits its log-symmetric center within half-unit rounding bounds.

### S001 — Year-7 HbA1c net change, percentage points

- **Source/role:** REPORT [DOC1 p6](../../../jama_courcoulas_2024_oi_240004_1721756962.76052.pdf#page=6), Table2 row 1; MAIN-076. Governing methods REPORT [DOC1 p3](../../../jama_courcoulas_2024_oi_240004_1721756962.76052.pdf#page=3) and [DOC3 p4](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=4).

- **Checks:** Shared row specification applied to all three tabulated intervals. Continuous group difference arithmetic reconciles with half-unit input/contrast intervals; binary group and within-year ORs use distinct contrasts, so an OR ratio is not substituted for the year-7 group OR. Matched abstract/p3 between-group −1.4[−1.8,−1.0],P<.001 agrees. The positive abstract/narrative surgery point1.6 lies outside its signed negative interval: F-ST1-009 under routeA1. A deliberate reduction-magnitude reading is preserved as an alternative. Planned scale comparison S064/F-ST1-002.
- **Named limits:** Covariance, numerical SE, exact df/test distribution and outcome-specific observed n are not printed. They are not inferred. Raw-to-adjusted differences are not findings.
- **Pass1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE
- **Pass2:** PASS_2_COMPLETE
- **Second-pass facts:** Table 2 signed changes −0.2 and −1.6 and group −1.4 [−1.8,−1.0], P<.001 reconcile for containment, direction, group subtraction and significance. Positive prose surgery 1.6 outside its negative interval remains C001; primary scale and covariate/sensitivity mapping remain C004/C007/C008. No new issue; covariance/df and exact fitted output remain absent.

# Statistical relationship inventory — second-pass shard S051–S100

Runtime agent: /root/statistical_pass2_main_extra; model gpt-6.1-sol; reasoning effort high; FRESH_SPAWN. Fresh second-pass agent distinct from the original first-pass agent. Exact disjoint scope: S051 through S100 (50 records).

Every original assigned relationship below is retained with PASS_1_COMPLETE. PASS_2_COMPLETE source facts and checks are added per record from checkers/parts/statistical_pass2_02.md. Complete current ledger C001–C014, all 24 screening decisions, mechanical recheck and all 53 original exclusions were read. Source-linked direct proof and current-run renderings were reused on CPU; no fresh extraction/OCR, external source, Python review code or amendment inference was introduced.

Shared checks: actual count/denominator and model/time matching; estimate containment and endpoint/direction order where supplied; interval/test compatibility where supplied; stated primary/multiplicity/stopping rules; model/design logic; full display precision; and planned/reported correspondence. One-SE bars are not confidence intervals. Unreported model details and non-key exploratory outputs are named limits, not assumptions. Global original-exclusion registration belongs to the main second-pass agent.

## Preserved original source/statistical definitions and calculation provenance


The supplied source is [DOC-003](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=17), REPORT by content. Direct Poppler layout and freshly rendered source pages were read; the support mapper transcription is a locator. Native direct extraction covers physical pp17-21 and the supporting method definitions on pp4-6. Source authority is the rendered PDF. All count, denominator, percent, P-value and reference-group cells on the four assigned table pages were visually confirmed.

DOC-003 [p6](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=6) states that adverse events and study outcomes were summarized as percentages and compared using chi-squared or Fisher’s exact tests; [p5](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=5) states secondary outcomes are hypothesis generating without multiple-testing adjustment. eTable5 footnote b on p20 and eTable6 footnote b on p21 repeat the chi-squared/Fisher family. eTable3 footnote a gives MED as comparison/reference and footnote b defines N as participants with observed data. No odds ratios, coefficients, CIs, standard errors, or test statistics are printed in these three tables. No regression/longitudinal adjustment is attributed to these table tests; no adjusted-versus-crude comparison is performed. Printed tests belong to through-12-year nutritional abnormalities, adverse events and procedures, respectively.

## Calculation and precision rules used for every record

For MED events a among n and comparison-group events b among m, the diagnostic non-continuity-corrected Pearson statistic is (n+m)[a(m-b)-(n-a)b]^2/[nm(a+b)(n+m-a-b)]. The reported-family diagnostic P is its chi-square(1) survival probability. Degrees of freedom 1 is a mathematical consequence of each reconstructed 2-by-2 table, not an inferred regression degree of freedom. The compatible Fisher diagnostic is the fixed-margin hypergeometric sum over tables no more probable than the observed table, with floating-point equality tolerance 1e-10 on log probabilities. Fisher sidedness and probability-ordering convention are not separately stated by the authors: this sum is a clearly labeled diagnostic permitted-family reconstruction that reproduces numerous rows, not an assertion about the authors’ exact software option. The Pearson tail was calculated in JavaScript using an erfc approximation with absolute numerical accuracy better than 1.5e-7; hypergeometric probabilities were evaluated by log-factorial sums. No continuity correction or effect model is assumed to be the actual author method. Both diagnostic P values are retained, and compatibility with at least one stated test family is sufficient to avoid an invented method conflict.

Counts represent integer participants and events: their half-unit rounding bands contain the printed integer as their only possible discrete value. Every observed percentage is recomputed as 100n/N and allowed its half-unit printed rounding band. Every numeric P value is allowed its own half-unit band; the remaining gap divided by printed P is recorded. Tiny last-digit gaps in 5 rows are below the scope’s 0.05 relative-discrepancy threshold. No interval-versus-P conflict exists because no intervals are printed here, and no effect-estimate/test conflict is inferred from such last-digit count-to-P diagnostics. Two all-zero eTable3 contrasts print NA; Pearson has a zero expected-event margin, and the absence of a numeric test is explicitly tracked.

Point-estimate containment, CI endpoint order, interval-versus-P agreement, coefficient sign, superiority, noninferiority, equivalence, multiplicity decision, interim and stopping checks are not applicable to these count/P-only tables unless a corresponding claim is supplied; none is supplied in this scope. Direction is retained as comparison-group frequency relative to MED; no printed narrative effect claim conflicts with the table. No sample-size calculation is printed in this scope. This prevents inventing absent model, covariance, estimand, sidedness or margin definitions.

## Preserved pass-1 calculation definitions and limitations

The following definitions originate from the pass-1 extra statistical shard. Its three-table scope is provenance; this part owns only the 26 explicitly enumerated relationships. Its numeric diagnostics are retained without redundant recalculation.

## Source and statistical definitions

The supplied source is [DOC-003](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=17), REPORT by content. Direct Poppler layout and freshly rendered source pages were read; the support mapper transcription is a locator. Native direct extraction covers physical pp17-21 and the supporting method definitions on pp4-6. Source authority is the rendered PDF. All count, denominator, percent, P-value and reference-group cells on the four assigned table pages were visually confirmed.

DOC-003 [p6](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=6) states that adverse events and study outcomes were summarized as percentages and compared using chi-squared or Fisher’s exact tests; [p5](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=5) states secondary outcomes are hypothesis generating without multiple-testing adjustment. eTable5 footnote b on p20 and eTable6 footnote b on p21 repeat the chi-squared/Fisher family. eTable3 footnote a gives MED as comparison/reference and footnote b defines N as participants with observed data. No odds ratios, coefficients, CIs, standard errors, or test statistics are printed in these three tables. No regression/longitudinal adjustment is attributed to these table tests; no adjusted-versus-crude comparison is performed. Printed tests belong to through-12-year nutritional abnormalities, adverse events and procedures, respectively.

## Calculation and precision rules used for every record

For MED events a among n and comparison-group events b among m, the diagnostic non-continuity-corrected Pearson statistic is (n+m)[a(m-b)-(n-a)b]^2/[nm(a+b)(n+m-a-b)]. The reported-family diagnostic P is its chi-square(1) survival probability. Degrees of freedom 1 is a mathematical consequence of each reconstructed 2-by-2 table, not an inferred regression degree of freedom. The compatible Fisher diagnostic is the fixed-margin hypergeometric sum over tables no more probable than the observed table, with floating-point equality tolerance 1e-10 on log probabilities. Fisher sidedness and probability-ordering convention are not separately stated by the authors: this sum is a clearly labeled diagnostic permitted-family reconstruction that reproduces numerous rows, not an assertion about the authors’ exact software option. The Pearson tail was calculated in JavaScript using an erfc approximation with absolute numerical accuracy better than 1.5e-7; hypergeometric probabilities were evaluated by log-factorial sums. No continuity correction or effect model is assumed to be the actual author method. Both diagnostic P values are retained, and compatibility with at least one stated test family is sufficient to avoid an invented method conflict.

Counts represent integer participants and events: their half-unit rounding bands contain the printed integer as their only possible discrete value. Every observed percentage is recomputed as 100n/N and allowed its half-unit printed rounding band. Every numeric P value is allowed its own half-unit band; the remaining gap divided by printed P is recorded. Tiny last-digit gaps in 5 rows are below the scope’s 0.05 relative-discrepancy threshold. No interval-versus-P conflict exists because no intervals are printed here, and no effect-estimate/test conflict is inferred from such last-digit count-to-P diagnostics. Two all-zero eTable3 contrasts print NA; Pearson has a zero expected-event margin, and the absence of a numeric test is explicitly tracked.

Point-estimate containment, CI endpoint order, interval-versus-P agreement, coefficient sign, superiority, noninferiority, equivalence, multiplicity decision, interim and stopping checks are not applicable to these count/P-only tables unless a corresponding claim is supplied; none is supplied in this scope. Direction is retained as comparison-group frequency relative to MED; no printed narrative effect claim conflicts with the table. No sample-size calculation is printed in this scope. This prevents inventing absent model, covariance, estimand, sidedness or margin definitions.

Native direct extraction covers physical pp17-21 and the supporting method definitions on pp4-6. Source authority is the rendered PDF. All count, denominator, percent, P-value and reference-group cells on the four assigned table pages were visually confirmed.

DOC-003 [p6](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=6) states that adverse events and study outcomes were summarized as percentages and compared using chi-squared or Fisher’s exact tests; [p5](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=5) states secondary outcomes are hypothesis generating without multiple-testing adjustment. eTable5 footnote b on p20 and eTable6 footnote b on p21 repeat the chi-squared/Fisher family. eTable3 footnote a gives MED as comparison/reference and footnote b defines N as participants with observed data. No odds ratios, coefficients, CIs, standard errors, or test statistics are printed in these three tables. No regression/longitudinal adjustment is attributed to these table tests; no adjusted-versus-crude comparison is performed. Printed tests belong to through-12-year nutritional abnormalities, adverse events and procedures, respectively.

## Calculation and precision rules used for every record

For MED events a among n and comparison-group events b among m, the diagnostic non-continuity-corrected Pearson statistic is (n+m)[a(m-b)-(n-a)b]^2/[nm(a+b)(n+m-a-b)]. The reported-family diagnostic P is its chi-square(1) survival probability. Degrees of freedom 1 is a mathematical consequence of each reconstructed 2-by-2 table, not an inferred regression degree of freedom. The compatible Fisher diagnostic is the fixed-margin hypergeometric sum over tables no more probable than the observed table, with floating-point equality tolerance 1e-10 on log probabilities. Fisher sidedness and probability-ordering convention are not separately stated by the authors: this sum is a clearly labeled diagnostic permitted-family reconstruction that reproduces numerous rows, not an assertion about the authors’ exact software option. The Pearson tail was calculated in JavaScript using an erfc approximation with absolute numerical accuracy better than 1.5e-7; hypergeometric probabilities were evaluated by log-factorial sums. No continuity correction or effect model is assumed to be the actual author method. Both diagnostic P values are retained, and compatibility with at least one stated test family is sufficient to avoid an invented method conflict.

Counts represent integer participants and events: their half-unit rounding bands contain the printed integer as their only possible discrete value. Every observed percentage is recomputed as 100n/N and allowed its half-unit printed rounding band. Every numeric P value is allowed its own half-unit band; the remaining gap divided by printed P is recorded. Tiny last-digit gaps in 5 rows are below the scope’s 0.05 relative-discrepancy threshold. No interval-versus-P conflict exists because no intervals are printed here, and no effect-estimate/test conflict is inferred from such last-digit count-to-P diagnostics. Two all-zero eTable3 contrasts print NA; Pearson has a zero expected-event margin, and the absence of a numeric test is explicitly tracked.

Point-estimate containment, CI endpoint order, interval-versus-P agreement, coefficient sign, superiority, noninferiority, equivalence, multiplicity decision, interim and stopping checks are not applicable to these count/P-only tables unless a corresponding claim is supplied; none is supplied in this scope. Direction is retained as comparison-group frequency relative to MED; no printed narrative effect claim conflicts with the table. No sample-size calculation is printed in this scope. This prevents inventing absent model, covariance, estimand, sidedness or margin definitions.

## Methods, evidence and pass-2 context

DOC-003 is REPORT by retrospective methods/results content. Source: [eTable 6, physical page 21](../../../joi240004supp2_prod_1721756962.82552.pdf#page=21). All cells and footnotes were visually reread in preprocessing/stat_extra_source21.png (current-run direct Poppler 26.01.0 rendering, CPU). Original direct-source methods on pages 5–6 were reread from preprocessing/screen_report_supp_direct.txt, produced by Poppler 26.01.0 pdftotext. Existing derivatives and supplied sources were unchanged; no OCR, new rendering, external literature, raw data or custom Python was used.

The printed method is chi-squared or Fisher's exact test, with secondary results hypothesis generating and unadjusted for multiple testing. The retained Pearson non-continuity-corrected and probability-ordering Fisher reconstructions are diagnostic calculations under that permitted family, not assertions of actual software settings. No Fisher sidedness/ordering, continuity-correction option or per-row selection rule is inferred. The original Pearson approximation accuracy bound is better than 1.5e-7; Fisher uses fixed-margin hypergeometric log-factorial sums. The original numeric proofs were reviewed and reused without unnecessary repeat calculation.

Every central percentage lies in its displayed half-unit interval; hence full rounding-input intervals also overlap. Each numeric P interval intersects one diagnostic and S198's literal P<.001 is compatible with both. These checks have zero interval gap and zero relative discrepancy. There are no effect CIs, coefficients, SEs or test statistics in this table, and no supplied per-row margin, interim/stopping or sample-size rule; absent quantities are tracked as not applicable rather than reconstructed from convention. None of these rows prints NA.

The complete scope/contract, packet_06 and shared ledger/screen/recheck/all 53 exclusions were read. The screened vascular/safety analysis mapping F-XS-010 and F-ST1-008 is preserved: compatible row diagnostics do not establish executed test identity or resolve absent CMH strata/test mapping. Other screened comparisons neither change these row inputs nor justify a new denominator or fitted estimand. All 25 assigned rows have no newly qualifying issue; no new apparent observation was excluded. Global exclusion-finding ownership remains with the primary pass-2 agent.
